5. If not already on the Registration page, click the 'Don't have an account?' link to navigate to the Registration page.
6. Type 'Jane' into the First Name field.
7. Type 'Doe' into the Last Name field.
8. Type 'test_TC_ACC_015_1779452744817@posters.com' into the Email field.
9. Type 'S3cur3!P@ss' into the Password field.
10. Click the 'Create Account' button.
11. Verify that you are registered successfully and logged in.
12. Click the 'Logout' button.
13. Click the profile/sign-in icon at the top right to go to the Sign In page.
